# Codex conversation

## User

A previous agent produced the plan below to accomplish the user's task. Implement the plan in a fresh context. Treat the plan as the source of user intent, re-read files as needed, and carry the work through implementation and verification.

# Parkour through the world

## Summary

Replace the central line of isolated jumps with **one winding route across actual buildings, trees, cliffs, and other structures**. Keep the beautiful scenery, partial symmetry, dragon chase, and three lives. Difficulty should come from varied movement and maintaining flow, with forgiving landings.

Each course still shuffles six different sections from the existing twelve. Buildings, trees, and waterfalls are possible sections, rather than mandatory in every run.

## Traversal design

Give each section an authored movement sequence instead of a random deck of pads:

| Section | Playable route |
|---|---|
| Rooftop village | Cross broad roofs, climb dormers, round chimneys, and jump between balconies. |
| Great trees | Ascend branches around a trunk, cross supported canopy platforms, and descend onto another tree. |
| Waterfall canyon | Follow cliff shelves, cross stepping stones rooted in river terraces, and pass behind a waterfall through a dry passage. |
| Castle ramparts | Run battlements, turn around towers, and pass through a gatehouse. |
| Rope bridge | Cross anchored bridge segments, stone abutments, and supported lookout platforms. |
| Aqueduct | Traverse the channel edges, arch crowns, and maintenance stairs. |
| Sky galleon | Move across the deck, raised cabins, mast platforms, and connected rigging walkways. |
| Clock towers | Ascend exterior balconies and cross a bridge between paired towers. |
| Crystal cavern | Follow rock shelves, tunnel openings, and substantial crystal formations. |
| Lantern shrine | Cross pond stones, temple steps, courtyards, and supported roof terraces. |
| Mine scaffold | Climb timber platforms, cross braced beams, and enter rock-cut passages. |
| Foundry | Traverse machinery platforms, furnace galleries, and bridges above molten channels. |

Mix continuous running, turns, short climbs, jumps, and controlled drops. Use wide landings at corners and checkpoints. Keep narrow beams and existing special pads as occasional accents.

## Implementation

- **Generate route and structure together.** In `src/parkour_course.ts`, replace the centre-line walk with deterministic section templates containing structure anchors, traversal surfaces, entrance/exit connections, and supported geometry. Each template has bounded seeded variations and a validated fallback within the same section.
- **Extend the shared course model** with walk/jump/impulse transitions, traversal timing, and structure/support ownership. Retain ordered progress and existing seed-based generation; client and server derive identical geometry without a new network payload.
- **Build complete playable structures.** Update `src/parkour_setpieces.ts` to consume that layout. Register traversable roof, branch, and shelf surfaces as route geometry. Preserve their supporting structures while keeping player headroom and jump paths clear. Remove the blanket rule that eliminates nearby standable scenery.
- **Keep visual balance without copying the route.** Use paired towers, balanced tree groups, canyon walls, and matching architectural details. Remove whole-venue mirroring that would overwrite an asymmetric route or create an unintended second route.
- **Attach everything convincingly.** Keep coherent floating islands and the sky ship. Roofs belong to buildings, branches connect to trunks, stones extend into riverbeds, and platforms have braces, posts, or suspension. Cleanup must preserve essential supports and remove loose decorative fragments.
- **Orient chains correctly.** Add X/Y/Z chain variants using rotated link geometry, keeping the existing chain ID for vertical chains and retaining non-colliding behavior. Horizontal chains connect visibly to anchors at both ends; hanging chains connect above and to their suspended object where applicable. Generate attachments with the chain so filtering cannot leave broken spans.
- **Update traversal consumers.** Bots walk continuous spans and plan jumps only where needed. Progress detection distinguishes nearby stages on winding or stacked routes while retaining legitimate jump skips and wool bailouts. Respawns use stable, unobstructed surfaces.
- **Retune the chase by traversal time.** Weight dragon advancement, surge distance, and respawn lead by expected traversal duration instead of treating every stage equally. Keep fractional course progress on the wire. Ensure flight paths clear structures and nearby folds of the route do not cause catches through walls.
- **Guide turns naturally.** Use lamps, material accents, and existing next-target markers to make the next movement visible, including behind-waterfall passages and tree climbs.

## Verification and acceptance

- Simulate every transition with real player physics against the completed venue across all themes and representative seeds.
- Verify supported route surfaces, chain orientation and endpoint attachment, clear jump paths, and safe two- and four-player spawns.
- Replace exact-mirror and scenery-exclusion tests with checks for intended structural balance, preserved supports, and unobstructed traversal.
- Test continuous progress, stacked routes, drops, checkpoints, special pads, wool bailouts, and dragon catches near route bends.
- Run `npm test`, `npm run build`, bot simulations, and the Worlds stress suite.
- Play through every section on desktop and touch controls. Review screenshots for readable routes, complete structures, partial symmetry, and correctly attached horizontal chains.

Success means each section feels like traversing a place, with a distinct movement rhythm—not another line of pads wearing different materials.

## Assistant

I’ll inspect the course generator, setpieces, and traversal code, then implement the authored routes and verify them against the existing tests and simulations.

    // The builds around a Dragon Chase course.
    //
    // parkour_course.ts lays the ROUTE: every pad and every jump. This file puts
    // a place round it — castle walls under the wall-walk, a village whose tower
    // roofs you hop, a galleon, a great tree, a clock tower, a cavern cut through a
    // mountain — one builder per set piece, plus the Dragon's Lair behind the start
    // and the Sanctuary (and its podium) at the finish.
    //
    // The look is CLEAN: straight faces, flat tops, smooth tiers, and everything
    // that comes in pairs mirrored across the route's centre line. No rubble, no
    // noise-chewed edges, nothing half-built.
    //
    // One rule keeps the route honest. Every build is checked against the
    // route's own boxes (`course.spaces`):
    //   · nothing solid within a block of a pad, or in or beside the air a jump
    //     flies through (from three below its lower end to five above its upper);
    //   · within five blocks sideways of the route, no solid block may leave a
    //     top you could stand on between two below the route and one above it.
    // A STRUCTURE (a tower, a house, a lamp post, a span) that breaks the rule
    // anywhere is left out whole — never shaved down into a broken stump. Only
    // terrain (islands, cliffs, the cavern's mountain) is cut to fit, and a last
    // sweep removes anything left floating on its own.
    import { Block, BLOCKS } from './blocks';
    import { partyHash } from './partygames';
    import { parkourCourse, type ParkourCourse, type ParkourPlatform, type ParkourSection } from './parkour_course';
    import {
      PARKOUR_CENTRE_X, PARKOUR_VENUE_X, PARKOUR_VENUE_Z, PARTY_STAMP_MAX_Y, PARTY_STAMP_MIN_Y,
    } from './venue_dims';
    import { parkourTheme, type ParkourPalette } from './parkour_themes';
    
    const X = PARKOUR_VENUE_X, Z = PARKOUR_VENUE_Z, CX = PARKOUR_CENTRE_X;
    const MIN_Y = PARTY_STAMP_MIN_Y, MAX_Y = PARTY_STAMP_MAX_Y, H = MAX_Y - MIN_Y + 1;
    /** Cells in one horizontal layer: stepping a key by this moves one block up. */
    const LAYER = X * Z;
    const key = (x: number, y: number, z: number): number => (y - MIN_Y) * LAYER + z * X + x;
    /** Blocks with collision (water counts: it changes how you move). */
    const PHYSICAL = new Uint8Array(256), SOLID = new Uint8Array(256);
    for (let i = 1; i < 256; i++) {
      SOLID[i] = BLOCKS[i]?.solid ? 1 : 0;
      PHYSICAL[i] = SOLID[i] || i === Block.Water ? 1 : 0;
    }
    /** Groups of touching blocks smaller than this, touching nothing on the
     *  route, are strays: swept away. */
    const STRAY_LIMIT = 40;
    
    // ── The stamp ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    /** A finished venue, packed as runs of one block per column: a mountain or a
     *  tower is a handful of bytes, not thousands of map entries. */
    export class ParkourVenue {
      constructor(private readonly colStart: Int32Array, private readonly runs: Uint8Array) { }
      get(x: number, y: number, z: number): number {
        if (x < 0 || x >= X || z < 0 || z >= Z || y < MIN_Y || y > MAX_Y) return Block.Air;
        const c = z * X + x, ry = y - MIN_Y;
        for (let i = this.colStart[c], end = this.colStart[c + 1]; i < end; i += 3) {
          const y0 = this.runs[i];
          if (ry < y0) return Block.Air;
          if (ry < y0 + this.runs[i + 1]) return this.runs[i + 2];
        }
        return Block.Air;
      }
      /** Blocks in the venue (tests). */
      count(): number {
        let n = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < this.runs.length; i += 3) if (this.runs[i + 2] !== Block.Air) n += this.runs[i + 1];
        return n;
      }
    }
    
    function pack(cells: Uint8Array): ParkourVenue {
      const colStart = new Int32Array(LAYER + 1);
      const out: number[] = [];
      for (let c = 0; c < LAYER; c++) {
        colStart[c] = out.length;
        for (let y = 0; y < H;) {
          const b = cells[y * LAYER + c];
          if (!b) { y++; continue; }
          let n = 1;
          while (y + n < H && cells[(y + n) * LAYER + c] === b && n < 255) n++;
          out.push(y, n, b);
          y += n;
        }
      }
      colStart[LAYER] = out.length;
      return new ParkourVenue(colStart, Uint8Array.from(out));
    }
    
    // ── Building helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    class Builder {
      /** The whole venue, one byte a cell. */
      readonly cells = new Uint8Array(LAYER * H);
      /** Which structure put each cell there (0 = terrain, which may be cut). */
      readonly owner = new Uint16Array(LAYER * H);
      private current = 0;
      private nextId = 1;
      constructor(readonly seed: number, readonly pal: ParkourPalette) { }
      /** Build `fn` as one structure: kept whole, or left out whole. */
      whole(fn: () => void): void {
        const was = this.current;
        this.current = this.nextId < 65535 ? this.nextId++ : 65535;
        try { fn(); } finally { this.current = was; }
      }
      inside(x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
        return x >= 0 && x < X && z >= 0 && z < Z && y >= MIN_Y + 1 && y <= MAX_Y;
      }
      put(x: number, y: number, z: number, b: number): void {
        x = Math.round(x); y = Math.round(y); z = Math.round(z);
        if (!this.inside(x, y, z)) return;
        const k = key(x, y, z);
        this.cells[k] = b;
        this.owner[k] = this.current;
      }
      /** Only into empty air. */
      add(x: number, y: number, z: number, b: number): void {
        x = Math.round(x); y = Math.round(y); z = Math.round(z);
        if (this.inside(x, y, z) && !this.cells[key(x, y, z)]) this.put(x, y, z, b);
      }
      get(x: number, y: number, z: number): number { return this.inside(x, y, z) ? this.cells[key(x, y, z)] : Block.Air; }
      clear(x: number, y: number, z: number): void { if (this.inside(x, y, z)) this.cells[key(x, y, z)] = Block.Air; }
      fill(x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number, z0: number, z1: number, b: number): void {
        for (let y = Math.min(y0, y1); y <= Math.max(y0, y1); y++)
          for (let z = Math.min(z0, z1); z <= Math.max(z0, z1); z++)
            for (let x = Math.min(x0, x1); x <= Math.max(x0, x1); x++) this.put(x, y, z, b);
      }
      /** Copy the west half of everything onto the east half, so the venue is
       *  an exact mirror image across the route's centre line. */
      mirror(): void {
        for (let y = MIN_Y + 1; y <= MAX_Y; y++)
          for (let z = 0; z < Z; z++) {
            const row = (y - MIN_Y) * LAYER + z * X;
            this.cells[row] = Block.Air;
            for (let x = 1; x < CX; x++) {
              this.cells[row + 2 * CX - x] = this.cells[row + x];
              this.owner[row + 2 * CX - x] = this.owner[row + x];
            }
          }
      }
      /** 0..1, stable for its arguments. */
      r(...n: number[]): number {
        let h = this.seed >>> 0;
        for (const v of n) h = partyHash(h, v | 0);
        return h / 4294967296;
      }
    
      /** A floating island: a soil-topped slab whose underside steps in, tier by
       *  tier, to a point. Clean edges; `top` is its surface height. */
      island(cx: number, cz: number, rx: number, rz: number, top: number, depth: number, soil = this.pal.soil): void {
        for (let z = Math.floor(cz - rz); z <= Math.ceil(cz + rz); z++)
          for (let x = Math.floor(cx - rx); x <= Math.ceil(cx + rx); x++) {
            const d = Math.hypot((x - cx) / rx, (z - cz) / rz);
            if (d > 1) continue;
            const under = Math.max(1, Math.round(depth * Math.sqrt(1 - d * d)));
            for (let y = top - under; y < top; y++) this.put(x, y, z, (top - y) % 4 === 3 ? this.pal.brick : this.pal.rock);
            this.put(x, top, z, soil);
          }
      }
    
      /** A tree of the palette's kind, rooted at (x, y, z). A neat round crown. */
      tree(x: number, y: number, z: number, size = 1): void {
        this.whole(() => {
          const leaves = this.pal.leaves;
          const species = leaves === Block.CherryLeaves ? 'cherry' : leaves === Block.SpruceLeaves ? 'spruce'
            : leaves === Block.BirchLeaves ? 'birch' : 'oak';
          const log = species === 'cherry' ? Block.CherryLog : species === 'spruce' ? Block.SpruceLog
            : species === 'birch' ? Block.BirchLog : Block.OakLog;
          const top = Math.round((species === 'spruce' ? 7 : species === 'cherry' ? 6 : 5) * size);
          const leafAt = (dx: number, dy: number, dz: number) => this.add(x + dx, y + dy, z + dz, leaves);
          if (species === 'spruce') {
            for (let dy = 2; dy <= top + 1; dy++) {
              const fromTop = top + 1 - dy;
              const radius = fromTop === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(3, 1 + Math.floor(fromTop / 2) - (fromTop % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 0));
              for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++)
                for (let dz = -radius; dz <= radius; dz++)
                  if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dz) <= radius + (radius > 1 ? 1 : 0)) leafAt(dx, dy, dz);
            }
            leafAt(0, top + 2, 0);
          } else {
            const wide = species === 'cherry' ? 3 * size : 2.4 * size;
            for (let dy = top - 2; dy <= top + 1; dy++) {
              const layer = dy - top;
              const radius = layer === 1 ? wide - 1.3 : layer === -2 ? wide - .7 : wide;
              const rr = Math.ceil(radius);
              for (let dx = -rr; dx <= rr; dx++)
                for (let dz = -rr; dz <= rr; dz++) if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) <= radius + .2) leafAt(dx, dy, dz);
            }
          }
          for (let dy = 0; dy < top; dy++) this.put(x, y + dy, z, log);
        });
      }
    
      /** A post with a lamp on it. */
      lampPost(x: number, y: number, z: number, h = 3): void {
        this.whole(() => {
          for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) this.put(x, y + dy, z, this.pal.log);
          this.put(x, y + h, z, this.pal.lamp);
        });
      }
    
      /** A banner of cloth hanging down from (x, y, z). */
      banner(x: number, y: number, z: number, len: number, cloth = this.pal.cloth): void {
        for (let dy = 0; dy < len; dy++) this.put(x, y - dy, z, cloth);
      }
    
      /** A round tower: `r` its radius, walls from y0 to y1, a crenellated top,
       *  slit windows, and (unless `flat`) a cone roof. */
      tower(cx: number, cz: number, r: number, y0: number, y1: number, opts: { flat?: boolean; roof?: number; wall?: number } = {}): void {
        this.whole(() => {
          const wall = opts.wall ?? this.pal.stone, roof = opts.roof ?? this.pal.roof;
          const rr = Math.ceil(r);
          for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++)
            for (let dz = -rr; dz <= rr; dz++)
              for (let dx = -rr; dx <= rr; dx++) {
                const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
                if (d > r + .3) continue;
                const shell = d > r - 1.1;
                if (!shell) { if (y === y1) this.put(cx + dx, y, cz + dz, this.pal.wood); continue; }
                const slit = (y - y0) % 5 === 3 && (dx === 0 || dz === 0);
                const band = (y - y0) % 6 === 0;
                this.put(cx + dx, y, cz + dz, slit ? this.pal.glow : band ? this.pal.brick : wall);
              }
          for (let dz = -rr - 1; dz <= rr + 1; dz++)
            for (let dx = -rr - 1; dx <= rr + 1; dx++) {
              const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
              if (d > r + 1.3 || d <= r - .2) continue;
              this.put(cx + dx, y1, cz + dz, this.pal.trim);
              if ((dx + dz) % 2 === 0) this.put(cx + dx, y1 + 1, cz + dz, this.pal.trim);
            }
          if (opts.flat) return;
          for (let k = 0; k <= r + 2; k++) {
            const rad = r + .5 - k * .8;
            if (rad < 0) { this.put(cx, y1 + 2 + k, cz, this.pal.trim); break; }
            const rc = Math.ceil(rad);
            for (let dz = -rc; dz <= rc; dz++)
              for (let dx = -rc; dx <= rc; dx++)
                if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) <= rad + .3) this.put(cx + dx, y1 + 2 + k, cz + dz, roof);
          }
        });
      }
    
      /** A house: stone plinth, framed walls with windows and a door, and a
       *  stepped gable roof along its longer side. (x0, z0) is a corner. */
      house(x0: number, z0: number, w: number, d: number, y0: number, wallH: number, roof = this.pal.roof, door: 'x0' | 'x1' = 'x1'): void {
        this.whole(() => {
          const x1 = x0 + w - 1, z1 = z0 + d - 1;
          for (let y = y0; y < y0 + wallH; y++)
            for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++)
              for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
                const edge = x === x0 || x === x1 || z === z0 || z === z1;
                if (!edge) continue;
                const corner = (x === x0 || x === x1) && (z === z0 || z === z1);
                const low = y - y0 < 2;
                const win = !corner && !low && (y - y0) % 3 === 2 && ((z - z0) % 2 === 1);
                this.put(x, y, z, corner ? this.pal.log : low ? this.pal.stone : win ? this.pal.glass : this.pal.trim);
              }
          // The door faces the street.
          const dx = door === 'x0' ? x0 : x1, dz = z0 + (d >> 1);
          this.put(dx, y0, dz, this.pal.wood); this.put(dx, y0 + 1, dz, this.pal.wood);
          const alongX = w >= d;
          const span = alongX ? d : w;
          const top = y0 + wallH;
          for (let k = 0; k <= (span + 1) >> 1; k++) {
            const y = top + k;
            if (alongX) {
              for (let x = x0 - 1; x <= x1 + 1; x++) { this.put(x, y, z0 - 1 + k, roof); this.put(x, y, z1 + 1 - k, roof); }
              for (const x of [x0, x1]) for (let z = z0 + k; z <= z1 - k; z++) this.put(x, y, z, this.pal.trim);
            } else {
              for (let z = z0 - 1; z <= z1 + 1; z++) { this.put(x0 - 1 + k, y, z, roof); this.put(x1 + 1 - k, y, z, roof); }
              for (const z of [z0, z1]) for (let x = x0 + k; x <= x1 - k; x++) this.put(x, y, z, this.pal.trim);
            }
          }
        });
      }
    
      /** A straight line of blocks from a to b (inclusive), `thick` wide. */
      line(ax: number, ay: number, az: number, bx: number, by: number, bz: number, block: number, thick = 0): void {
        const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(bx - ax), Math.abs(by - ay), Math.abs(bz - az)) * 2));
        for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
          const t = i / n, x = Math.round(ax + (bx - ax) * t), y = Math.round(ay + (by - ay) * t), z = Math.round(az + (bz - az) * t);
          for (let dx = -thick; dx <= thick; dx++) for (let dz = -thick; dz <= thick; dz++) this.put(x + dx, y, z + dz, block);
        }
      }
      /** A chain hanging from (x, y, z) down `len` blocks. */
      chain(x: number, y: number, z: number, len: number): void {
        for (let dy = 0; dy < len; dy++) this.add(x, y - dy, z, Block.ParkourChain);
      }
      /** A cliff or rock wall: a straight face at `face` (the side toward the
       *  route), `thick` deep, flat-topped at `top`, in neat strata. */
      wall(faceX: number, dir: -1 | 1, thick: number, z0: number, z1: number, y0: number, top: number): void {
        for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++)
          for (let y = y0; y <= top; y++)
            for (let k = 0; k < thick; k++) {
              const x = faceX + dir * k;
              this.put(x, y, z, y === top ? this.pal.soil : (top - y) % 5 === 4 ? this.pal.brick : this.pal.rock);
            }
      }
    }
    
    // ── What each piece is given ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    interface Area {
      index: number;
      sec: ParkourSection;
      pads: ParkourPlatform[];
      /** Pad extents, venue-local. */
      x0: number; x1: number; z0: number; z1: number;
      /** Lowest and highest standing heights. */
      lo: number; hi: number;
    }
    
    const cellOf = (p: ParkourPlatform) => ({ x: Math.floor(p.x), z: Math.floor(p.z) });
    /** Footprint of a pad, inclusive. */
    function foot(p: ParkourPlatform): { x0: number; x1: number; z0: number; z1: number } {
      const { x, z } = cellOf(p);
      const hx = (p.width - 1) / 2, hz = (p.depth - 1) / 2;
      return { x0: x - hx, x1: x + hx, z0: z - hz, z1: z + hz };
    }
    /** The highest standing height of the pads near venue-local z. */
    function localHigh(a: Area, z: number): number {
      let best = -Infinity;
      for (const p of a.pads) if (Math.abs(p.z - z) <= 8) best = Math.max(best, p.y);
      return Number.isFinite(best) ? best : a.hi;
    }
    /** Fill under a pad's footprint from just below its surface down to `bottom`
     *  (terrain-like: cut where it has to be, never a structure of its own). */
    function pier(b: Builder, p: ParkourPlatform, bottom: number, block: number, band?: number): void {
      const f = foot(p);
      for (let y = p.y - 2; y >= bottom; y--)
        b.fill(f.x0, f.x1, y, y, f.z0, f.z1, band !== undefined && (p.y - y) % 5 === 0 ? band : block);
    }
    /** Every jump inside a piece, as [from, to]. */
    function jumps(a: Area, course: ParkourCourse): [ParkourPlatform, ParkourPlatform][] {
      return a.pads.filter(p => p.from >= 0).map(p => [course.platforms[p.from], p]);
    }
    /** Mirror an x across the route's centre line. */
    const mirror = (x: number): number => 2 * CX - x;
    
    // ── The set pieces ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    type PieceBuilder = (b: Builder, a: Area, course: ParkourCourse) => void;
    
    const ramparts: PieceBuilder = (b, a, course) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 11;
      b.island(CX, (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, 22, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6, ground, 7);
      // The wall: a pier under every pad and, between each pair, a stretch of
      // lower wall-walk three below you — the drop you are jumping over.
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.stone, P.brick);
      for (const [f, t] of jumps(a, course)) b.whole(() => {
        const m = Math.min(f.y, t.y), A = cellOf(f), B = cellOf(t);
        const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.hypot(B.x - A.x, B.z - A.z)));
        for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
          const x = Math.round(A.x + (B.x - A.x) * i / n), z = Math.round(A.z + (B.z - A.z) * i / n);
          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
            for (let y = ground + 1; y <= m - 4; y++) b.put(x + dx, y, z, y === m - 4 ? P.trim : P.stone);
        }
      });
      // Battlement posts either side of each pad: you run between them.
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        if (p.checkpoint) continue;
        const f = foot(p), z = cellOf(p).z;
        for (const x of [f.x0 - 2, f.x1 + 2]) b.whole(() => {
          for (let y = ground + 1; y <= p.y + 1; y++) b.put(x, y, z, y >= p.y ? P.trim : P.stone);
        });
      }
      // Towers in mirrored pairs down both flanks, flying banners.
      let pair = 0;
      for (let z = a.z0 + 6; z < a.z1; z += 22, pair++) {
        const top = localHigh(a, z) + 6;
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
          const x = CX + side * 15;
          b.tower(x, z, 3, ground + 1, top, { roof: pair % 2 ? P.roof : P.roof2 });
          b.whole(() => {
            b.banner(x - side * 4, top - 1, z, 5);
            b.put(x, top + 8, z, P.log); b.put(x, top + 9, z, P.log);
            b.put(x + side, top + 9, z, P.cloth); b.put(x + 2 * side, top + 9, z, P.cloth);
          });
        }
      }
      // A gatehouse arching high over the middle of the wall.
      const gz = Math.round((a.z0 + a.z1) / 2), gh = localHigh(a, gz);
      b.whole(() => {
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
          const x = CX + side * 10;
          b.fill(x - 2, x + 2, ground + 1, gh + 9, gz - 2, gz + 2, P.stone);
          b.fill(x - 2, x + 2, gh + 10, gh + 10, gz - 2, gz + 2, P.trim);
          for (const dx of [-2, 0, 2]) for (const dz of [-2, 2]) b.put(x + dx, gh + 11, gz + dz, P.trim);
          b.put(x, gh + 4, gz - 3, P.lamp);
        }
        b.fill(CX - 9, CX + 9, gh + 8, gh + 9, gz - 1, gz + 1, P.brick);
        b.fill(CX - 9, CX + 9, gh + 10, gh + 10, gz - 1, gz + 1, P.trim);
        b.banner(CX, gh + 9, gz - 2, 2, P.cloth2);
      });
    };
    
    const rooftops: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 12;
      b.island(CX, (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, 32, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6, ground, 6, Block.ManorPaving);
      // Every pad is the roof terrace of a tall, narrow house: the core under it
      // always stands; the wider storeys and skirt are added only where they fit.
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        const f = foot(p), eave = p.y - 5, roof = p.index % 2 ? P.roof : P.roof2;
        pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.trim);
        b.whole(() => {
          for (let y = ground + 1; y < eave; y++)
            for (let z = f.z0 - 2; z <= f.z1 + 2; z++)
              for (let x = f.x0 - 2; x <= f.x1 + 2; x++) {
                const edge = x === f.x0 - 2 || x === f.x1 + 2 || z === f.z0 - 2 || z === f.z1 + 2;
                if (!edge) continue;
                const corner = (x === f.x0 - 2 || x === f.x1 + 2) && (z === f.z0 - 2 || z === f.z1 + 2);
                const win = !corner && y - ground > 2 && (y - ground) % 3 === 0 && (x + z) % 2 === 0;
                b.put(x, y, z, corner ? P.log : y - ground <= 2 ? P.stone : win ? P.glass : P.trim);
              }
          b.fill(f.x0 - 2, f.x1 + 2, eave, eave, f.z0 - 2, f.z1 + 2, roof);
          b.fill(f.x0 - 1, f.x1 + 1, eave + 1, eave + 1, f.z0 - 1, f.z1 + 1, roof);
        });
      }
      // The rest of the village: mirrored rows of houses facing the street.
      for (let z = a.z0 - 2, i = 0; z < a.z1; z += 11, i++) {
        const w = 7 + (i % 2) * 2, d = 7, h = 6 + (i % 3) * 3;
        const roof = i % 2 ? P.roof : P.roof2;
        b.house(CX - 24 - w, z, w, d, ground + 1, h, roof, 'x1');
        b.house(mirror(CX - 24 - w) - w + 1, z, w, d, ground + 1, h, roof, 'x0');
        // Every third row, a washing line across the street between two poles.
        if (i % 3 === 1) b.whole(() => {
          const y = localHigh(a, z) + 7;
          for (const x of [CX - 21, CX + 21]) for (let yy = ground + 1; yy <= y; yy++) b.put(x, yy, z + 3, P.log);
          for (let x = CX - 20; x <= CX + 20; x++) {
            b.put(x, y, z + 3, Block.ParkourChain);
            if (x % 4 === 0) b.put(x, y - 1, z + 3, x % 8 ? P.cloth : P.cloth2);
          }
        });
      }
      // Street lamps and market stalls, in pairs.
      for (let z = a.z0, i = 0; z < a.z1; z += 7, i++) {
        for (const x of [CX - 18, CX + 18]) b.lampPost(x, ground + 1, z, 3);
        if (i % 2 === 0) for (const sx of [CX - 15, mirror(CX - 15) - 2]) b.whole(() => {
          for (const [dx, dz] of [[0, 0], [2, 0], [0, 2], [2, 2]]) b.fill(sx + dx, sx + dx, ground + 1, ground + 2, z + dz, z + dz, P.log);
          b.fill(sx - 1, sx + 3, ground + 3, ground + 3, z - 1, z + 3, i % 4 ? P.cloth : P.cloth2);
          b.put(sx + 1, ground + 1, z + 1, Block.ManorCask);
        });
      }
    };
    
    const ropebridge: PieceBuilder = (b, a, course) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 16, top = a.hi + 5;
      // The gorge: two straight, flat-topped cliffs facing each other.
      b.wall(CX - 9, -1, 12, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, ground, top);
      b.wall(CX + 9, 1, 12, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, ground, top);
      for (let z = a.z0; z <= a.z1; z += 12) for (const x of [CX - 15, CX + 15]) b.tree(x, top + 1, z, 1);
      // A river along the gorge floor.
      b.fill(CX - 8, CX + 8, ground, ground, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, P.liquid);
      b.fill(CX - 8, CX + 8, ground - 1, ground - 1, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, P.rock);
      // The bridge: a post either side of every plank and handrails between.
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        const f = foot(p), z = cellOf(p).z;
        for (const x of [f.x0 - 2, f.x1 + 2]) b.whole(() => { for (let y = ground + 1; y <= p.y + 1; y++) b.put(x, y, z, P.log); });
        pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.log, P.wood);
      }
      for (const [f, t] of jumps(a, course)) {
        const fa = foot(f), ta = foot(t), y = Math.min(f.y, t.y) + 1;
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) b.whole(() => {
          const fx = side < 0 ? fa.x0 - 2 : fa.x1 + 2, tx = side < 0 ? ta.x0 - 2 : ta.x1 + 2;
          b.line(fx, y, cellOf(f).z, tx, y, cellOf(t).z, P.wood);
        });
      }
    };
    
    const aqueduct: PieceBuilder = (b, a, course) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 15;
      b.island(CX, (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, 26, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6, ground, 6);
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.stone, P.brick);
        const f = foot(p);
        b.whole(() => b.fill(f.x0 - 1, f.x1 + 1, p.y - 5, p.y - 5, f.z0 - 1, f.z1 + 1, P.trim));
      }
      // Arched spans carrying a water channel between the piers.
      for (const [f, t] of jumps(a, course)) b.whole(() => {
        const m = Math.min(f.y, t.y), A = cellOf(f), B = cellOf(t);
        const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.hypot(B.x - A.x, B.z - A.z)));
        for (let k = 0; k <= n; k++) {
          const s = k / n, x = Math.round(A.x + (B.x - A.x) * s), z = Math.round(A.z + (B.z - A.z) * s);
          const depth = Math.round(4 * (1 - Math.sin(Math.PI * s)));
          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            b.put(x + dx, m - 6, z, P.stone);
            for (let y = m - 7; y >= m - 7 - depth; y--) b.put(x + dx, y, z, P.brick);
          }
          b.put(x - 1, m - 5, z, P.trim); b.put(x + 1, m - 5, z, P.trim);
          b.put(x, m - 5, z, P.liquid);
        }
      });
      // A lower arcade across the valley floor, and colonnades either side.
      for (let z = a.z0; z <= a.z1; z += 6) b.whole(() => {
        for (const x of [CX - 12, CX + 11]) b.fill(x, x + 1, ground + 1, ground + 7, z, z + 1, P.stone);
        b.fill(CX - 12, CX + 12, ground + 8, ground + 8, z, z + 1, P.brick);
      });
      for (let z = a.z0 + 2, i = 0; z < a.z1; z += 8, i++)
        for (const x of [CX - 18, CX + 18]) {
          if (i % 2) b.tree(x + Math.sign(x - CX) * 3, ground + 1, z, 1);
          else b.whole(() => { b.fill(x, x, ground + 1, ground + 6, z, z, P.trim); b.fill(x - 1, x + 1, ground + 7, ground + 7, z - 1, z + 1, P.trim); });
        }
    };
    
    const galleon: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal;
      const zs = a.z0 + 1, ze = a.z1 - 1, zc = (zs + ze) / 2, half = (ze - zs) / 2;
      const deck = a.lo - 5, hull = P.log, stripe = P.cloth2;
      // The hull: widest amidships, drawn in at bow and stern, rounded below.
      for (let z = zs; z <= ze; z++) {
        const u = (z - zc) / half, w = Math.max(1.5, 7 * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - u ** 4)));
        for (let k = 0; k <= 8; k++) {
          const hw = Math.round(w * (1 - (k / 9) ** 1.6));
          if (hw < 0) break;
          for (let dx = -hw; dx <= hw; dx++) {
            const skin = Math.abs(dx) >= hw - 1 || k === 8;
            if (k === 0) b.put(CX + dx, deck, z, Math.abs(dx) === hw ? hull : P.wood);
            else if (skin) b.put(CX + dx, deck - k, z, k === 2 ? stripe : k === 8 ? P.log : hull);
          }
        }
        const hw = Math.round(w);
        b.put(CX - hw, deck + 1, z, P.wood); b.put(CX + hw, deck + 1, z, P.wood);
        if (z % 4 === 0 && hw > 3) { b.put(CX - hw - 1, deck - 1, z, P.metal); b.put(CX + hw + 1, deck - 1, z, P.metal); }
      }
      b.whole(() => { b.line(CX, deck + 1, ze, CX, deck + 5, ze + 7, P.log); b.put(CX, deck + 6, ze + 7, P.glow); });
      // Twin masts either side of the route, with yards and sails high overhead.
      for (const mz of [zc - half * .55, zc, zc + half * .55].map(Math.round)) {
        const top = localHigh(a, mz) + 13;
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) b.whole(() => {
          const mx = CX + side * 9;
          for (let y = deck + 1; y <= top; y++) b.put(mx, y, mz, P.log);
          for (const yy of [top - 7, top - 3]) {
            b.line(mx - 5, yy, mz, mx + 5, yy, mz, P.log);
            for (let x = mx - 4; x <= mx + 4; x++)
              for (let y = yy - 3; y < yy; y++) if (x !== mx) b.put(x, y, mz + 1, stripe);
          }
          b.fill(mx - 1, mx + 1, top + 1, top + 1, mz - 1, mz + 1, P.wood);
          b.put(mx, top + 2, mz, P.lamp);
          b.put(mx, top + 3, mz, P.log); b.put(mx, top + 4, mz, P.log);
          b.banner(mx + side, top + 4, mz, 2, P.cloth);
        });
      }
      // Crate and mast stacks: the supports under every pad.
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        const f = foot(p), thin = p.width === 1 && p.depth === 1;
        for (let y = p.y - 2; y > deck; y--) b.fill(f.x0, f.x1, y, y, f.z0, f.z1, thin ? P.log : (p.y - y) % 3 === 0 ? P.log : P.wood);
      }
      for (let z = zs + 4; z < ze - 3; z += 6) for (const x of [CX - 5, CX + 5]) b.whole(() => b.put(x, deck + 1, z, Block.ManorCask));
    };
    
    const greattree: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 10, top = a.hi + 8;
      const log = P.leaves === Block.CherryLeaves ? Block.CherryLog : P.leaves === Block.SpruceLeaves ? Block.SpruceLog
        : P.leaves === Block.BirchLeaves ? Block.BirchLog : Block.OakLog;
      const zc = Math.round((a.z0 + a.z1) / 2);
      b.island(CX, zc, 30, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6, ground, 8);
      // Every pad stands on a trunk of its own.
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground + 1, log);
      // Two giant trees either side, their crowns meeting high over the route.
      for (const tz of [a.z0 + Math.round((a.z1 - a.z0) / 4), a.z1 - Math.round((a.z1 - a.z0) / 4)]) {
        const tx = CX - 15;
        for (let y = ground - 2; y <= top; y++) {
          const r = y <= ground + 1 ? 5.2 : y <= ground + 3 ? 4.2 : 3.2;
          for (let dz = -6; dz <= 6; dz++) for (let dx = -6; dx <= 6; dx++)
            if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) <= r) b.put(tx + dx, y, tz + dz, log);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
          const ang = i / 8 * Math.PI * 2;
          b.line(tx, ground + 1, tz, tx + Math.cos(ang) * 10, ground, tz + Math.sin(ang) * 10, log);
        }
        // A bough reaching in over the route and up into the crown.
        b.line(tx + 3, top - 2, tz, CX - 4, top + 3, tz, log, 0);
        const cy = top + 5;
        for (let y = cy - 4; y <= cy + 5; y++)
          for (let dz = -14; dz <= 14; dz++)
            for (let dx = -16; dx <= 16; dx++)
              if (Math.hypot(dx / 16, (y - cy) / 5.5, dz / 14) <= 1) b.put(tx + 4 + dx, y, tz + dz, P.leaves);
        for (let i = -2; i <= 2; i++) b.whole(() => { b.chain(tx + 8, cy - 5, tz + i * 5, 4); b.put(tx + 8, cy - 9, tz + i * 5, P.lamp); });
      }
      for (let z = a.z0 + 2; z < a.z1; z += 9) b.tree(CX - 25, ground + 1, z, .8);
    };
    
    const clocktower: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 10, top = a.hi + 12;
      const cz = Math.round((a.z0 + a.z1) / 2);
      b.island(CX, cz, 28, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6, ground, 7, Block.ManorPaving);
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.stone, P.brick);
      // Twin clock towers either side, joined by an arch high over the route.
      const hx = 4, hz = 5, tx = CX - 14;
      b.whole(() => {
        for (let y = ground + 1; y <= top; y++)
          for (let z = cz - hz; z <= cz + hz; z++)
            for (let x = tx - hx; x <= tx + hx; x++) {
              const edge = x === tx - hx || x === tx + hx || z === cz - hz || z === cz + hz;
              if (!edge) continue;
              const corner = (x === tx - hx || x === tx + hx) && (z === cz - hz || z === cz + hz);
              const win = !corner && (y - ground) % 4 === 2 && (z === cz || x === tx);
              b.put(x, y, z, corner ? P.trim : (y - ground) % 6 === 0 ? P.brick : win ? P.glass : P.stone);
            }
        // Clock faces on the three outer sides and toward the route.
        const fy = top - 4;
        const face = (at: (u: number, v: number) => [number, number, number]) => {
          for (let v = -2; v <= 2; v++) for (let u = -2; u <= 2; u++) {
            if (Math.abs(u) === 2 && Math.abs(v) === 2) continue;
            const [x, y, z] = at(u, v);
            b.put(x, y, z, u === 0 && v === 0 ? P.glow : P.trim);
          }
          const [hx0, hy0, hz0] = at(0, 1), [hx1, hy1, hz1] = at(1, 0);
          b.put(hx0, hy0, hz0, P.metal); b.put(hx1, hy1, hz1, P.metal);
        };
        face((u, v) => [tx + u, fy + v, cz - hz - 1]);
        face((u, v) => [tx - u, fy + v, cz + hz + 1]);
        face((u, v) => [tx - hx - 1, fy + v, cz + u]);
        face((u, v) => [tx + hx + 1, fy + v, cz - u]);
        for (let k = 0; k <= hx + 1; k++)
          b.fill(tx - (hx + 1 - k), tx + (hx + 1 - k), top + 1 + k, top + 1 + k, cz - (hz + 1 - k), cz + (hz + 1 - k), P.roof2);
        b.fill(tx, tx, top + hx + 3, top + hx + 5, cz, cz, P.trim);
        b.put(tx, top + hx + 6, cz, P.lamp);
        // The arch: from this tower to the centre line (the mirror completes it).
        b.fill(tx + hx + 1, CX, top - 1, top, cz - 1, cz + 1, P.brick);
        b.fill(tx + hx + 1, CX, top + 1, top + 1, cz - 1, cz + 1, P.trim);
        for (let x = tx + hx + 2; x <= CX; x += 2) b.put(x, top + 2, cz, P.trim);
      });
      for (let z = a.z0 + 2; z < a.z1; z += 8) b.lampPost(CX - 22, ground + 1, z, 3);
    };
    
    const cavern: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 10, top = (a.sec.ceiling ?? a.hi + 16) - 5;
      // A whole mountain in smooth terraces; the route's boxes cut its passage.
      const zc = (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, rz = (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 2;
      for (let z = a.z0 + 3; z <= a.z1 - 1; z++)
        for (let x = CX - 22; x <= CX + 22; x++) {
          const d = Math.hypot((x - CX) / 22, (z - zc) / rz);
          if (d > 1) continue;
          const h = ground + Math.round((top - ground) * Math.min(1, (1 - d * d) * 1.6) / 2) * 2;
          for (let y = ground - Math.round(4 * (1 - d)); y <= h; y++)
            b.put(x, y, z, y === h ? P.soil : (h - y) % 5 === 4 ? P.brick : P.rock);
        }
      // Crystal spires in a line along the ridge.
      for (let z = a.z0 + 10; z < a.z1 - 6; z += 12)
        for (const x of [CX - 7, CX + 7]) b.whole(() => {
          let y = top;
          while (y > ground && !SOLID[b.get(x, y, z)]) y--;
          for (let k = 1; k <= 5; k++) b.put(x, y + k, z, Block.ParkourCrystalBlock);
          for (const [dx, dz] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) { b.put(x + dx, y + 1, z + dz, Block.ParkourCrystalBlock); b.put(x + dx, y + 2, z + dz, Block.ParkourCrystalBlock); }
        });
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground, P.rock, P.brick);
    };
    
    const waterfall: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 14, face = CX - 10, top = a.hi + 8;
      // A canyon: two straight cliff walls with falls pouring down them into a
      // river along the floor.
      b.wall(face, -1, 14, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, ground, top);
      for (let f = a.z0 + 8; f < a.z1 - 4; f += 16) {
        for (let z = f - 1; z <= f + 1; z++) for (let x = face - 3; x <= face; x++) for (let y = ground + 1; y <= top; y++) b.clear(x, y, z);
        for (let y = top; y > ground; y--) for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) b.put(face - 2, y, f + dz, P.liquid);
      }
      for (let z = a.z0; z <= a.z1; z += 10) b.tree(face - 8, top + 1, z, 1);
      b.fill(face + 1, CX, ground - 1, ground - 1, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, P.rock);
      b.fill(face + 1, CX, ground, ground, a.z0 - 6, a.z1 + 6, P.liquid);
      // Rock pillars under the ledges, rising out of the river.
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground, P.rock, P.brick);
    };
    
    const shrine: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 6;
      const zc = (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, rz = (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 6;
      b.island(CX, zc, 32, rz, ground, 7);
      // A pond under the whole route, stepping stones rising out of it.
      for (let z = a.z0 - 3; z <= a.z1 + 3; z++)
        for (let x = CX - 10; x <= CX + 10; x++) {
          if (Math.hypot((x - CX) / 10, (z - zc) / (rz - 2)) > 1) continue;
          b.put(x, ground, z, P.liquid);
          b.put(x, ground - 1, z, P.rock);
        }
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground - 1, P.trim);
      // Torii gates spanning the route.
      for (let z = a.z0 + 8; z < a.z1 - 4; z += 16) b.whole(() => {
        const hi = localHigh(a, z);
        for (const x of [CX - 8, CX + 8]) for (let y = ground; y <= hi + 7; y++) b.put(x, y, z, P.cloth);
        b.fill(CX - 8, CX + 8, hi + 7, hi + 7, z, z, P.cloth);
        b.fill(CX - 10, CX + 10, hi + 9, hi + 9, z, z, P.roof2);
        b.put(CX - 10, hi + 10, z, P.roof2); b.put(CX + 10, hi + 10, z, P.roof2);
        b.put(CX, hi + 8, z, P.trim);
      });
      // Stone lanterns round the pond.
      for (let z = a.z0; z <= a.z1; z += 6)
        for (const x of [CX - 13, CX + 13]) b.whole(() => {
          b.put(x, ground + 1, z, P.trim); b.put(x, ground + 2, z, P.trim);
          b.put(x, ground + 3, z, P.lamp);
          b.fill(x - 1, x + 1, ground + 4, ground + 4, z - 1, z + 1, P.roof2);
        });
      // A pagoda on each bank.
      const pz = Math.round(zc);
      for (const px of [CX - 23, CX + 23]) b.whole(() => {
        let y = ground + 1;
        for (let tier = 0; tier < 4; tier++) {
          const r = 4 - tier;
          for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++, y++)
            for (let dz = -r; dz <= r; dz++) for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
              if (Math.abs(dx) !== r && Math.abs(dz) !== r) continue;
              const corner = Math.abs(dx) === r && Math.abs(dz) === r;
              b.put(px + dx, y, pz + dz, corner ? P.cloth : k === 2 && (dx === 0 || dz === 0) ? P.glass : P.wood);
            }
          b.fill(px - r - 2, px + r + 2, y, y, pz - r - 2, pz + r + 2, P.roof2);
          for (const [dx, dz] of [[-r - 2, -r - 2], [r + 2, -r - 2], [-r - 2, r + 2], [r + 2, r + 2]]) b.put(px + dx, y + 1, pz + dz, P.roof2);
          y++;
        }
        b.fill(px, px, y, y + 3, pz, pz, P.trim);
        b.put(px, y + 4, pz, P.lamp);
      });
      for (let z = a.z0 + 3; z < a.z1; z += 14) for (const x of [CX - 16, CX + 16]) b.tree(x, ground + 1, z, 1);
    };
    
    const mine: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 10, top = a.hi + 9;
      b.island(CX, (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, 24, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 5, ground, 6, P.rock);
      // Mountain walls either side, each with a timbered mine mouth.
      const mz = Math.round((a.z0 + a.z1) / 2);
      b.wall(CX - 20, -1, 12, a.z0 - 4, a.z1 + 4, ground - 3, top);
      for (let z = mz - 2; z <= mz + 2; z++) for (let x = CX - 27; x <= CX - 20; x++) for (let y = ground + 1; y <= ground + 5; y++) b.clear(x, y, z);
      b.whole(() => {
        for (const dz of [-3, 3]) b.fill(CX - 20, CX - 20, ground + 1, ground + 6, mz + dz, mz + dz, P.log);
        b.fill(CX - 20, CX - 20, ground + 6, ground + 6, mz - 3, mz + 3, P.log);
        b.put(CX - 19, ground + 5, mz, P.lamp);
      });
      // Rails from each mine mouth along the valley floor, with carts.
      for (let z = a.z0; z <= a.z1; z++) { b.put(CX - 15, ground + 1, z, P.metal); b.put(CX - 13, ground + 1, z, P.metal); }
      b.fill(CX - 19, CX - 16, ground + 1, ground + 1, mz - 1, mz + 1, P.metal);
      for (let z = a.z0 + 5; z < a.z1; z += 17) b.whole(() => {
        b.fill(CX - 15, CX - 13, ground + 2, ground + 3, z, z + 2, P.wood);
        b.fill(CX - 14, CX - 14, ground + 3, ground + 3, z, z + 2, P.glow);
      });
      // Timber scaffolding under every pad: corner posts and cross-braces.
      for (const p of a.pads) {
        const f = foot(p);
        const corners: [number, number][] = p.width === 1 ? [[f.x0, f.z0], [f.x0, f.z1]]
          : [[f.x0, f.z0], [f.x1, f.z0], [f.x0, f.z1], [f.x1, f.z1]];
        for (const [x, z] of corners) for (let y = p.y - 2; y > ground; y--) b.put(x, y, z, P.log);
        for (let y = p.y - 4; y > ground + 1; y -= 4) {
          b.fill(f.x0, f.x1, y, y, f.z0, f.z0, P.wood); b.fill(f.x0, f.x1, y, y, f.z1, f.z1, P.wood);
          b.fill(f.x0, f.x0, y, y, f.z0, f.z1, P.wood); b.fill(f.x1, f.x1, y, y, f.z0, f.z1, P.wood);
        }
      }
      for (let z = a.z0 + 2; z < a.z1; z += 8) b.lampPost(CX - 17, ground + 1, z, 3);
    };
    
    const foundry: PieceBuilder = (b, a) => {
      const P = b.pal, ground = a.lo - 9;
      b.island(CX, (a.z0 + a.z1) / 2, 26, (a.z1 - a.z0) / 2 + 5, ground, 6, P.rock);
      // A molten channel under the route.
      for (let z = a.z0 - 4; z <= a.z1 + 4; z++) {
        b.fill(CX - 3, CX + 3, ground, ground, z, z, P.liquid === Block.Water ? Block.FurnaceCeramic : P.liquid);
        b.put(CX - 4, ground + 1, z, P.metal); b.put(CX + 4, ground + 1, z, P.metal);
      }
      // Furnaces either side, their chimneys and a gantry between them.
      for (let z = a.z0 + 4; z < a.z1; z += 26) {
        const hi = localHigh(a, z);
        for (const side of [-1, 1]) b.whole(() => {
          const x = CX + side * 17;
          for (let y = ground + 1; y <= hi + 6; y++)
            for (let dz = -3; dz <= 3; dz++) for (let dx = -3; dx <= 3; dx++) {
              if (Math.abs(dx) !== 3 && Math.abs(dz) !== 3) continue;
              const win = (y - ground) % 5 === 3 && (dx === 0 || dz === 0);
              b.put(x + dx, y, z + dz, win ? P.glow : (y - ground) % 5 === 0 ? P.trim : P.brick);
            }
          b.fill(x - 3, x + 3, hi + 7, hi + 7, z - 3, z + 3, P.trim);
          for (let y = hi + 8; y <= hi + 20; y++) b.fill(x - 1, x + 1, y, y, z - 1, z + 1, y % 4 === 0 ? P.trim : P.stone);
        });
        b.whole(() => {
          b.fill(CX - 14, CX + 14, hi + 8, hi + 8, z, z, P.metal);
          b.fill(CX - 14, CX + 14, hi + 9, hi + 9, z, z, P.trim);
        });
      }
      for (const p of a.pads) pier(b, p, ground + 1, P.metal, P.trim);
      for (let z = a.z0; z < a.z1; z += 8) b.whole(() => b.fill(CX - 11, CX - 10, ground + 1, ground + 2, z, z + 1, P.metal));
    };
    
    const BUILDERS: Record<ParkourSection['piece'], PieceBuilder> = {
      ramparts, rooftops, ropebridge, aqueduct, galleon, greattree, clocktower, cavern, waterfall, shrine, mine, foundry,
    };
    
    // ── The route's clearance ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    /** Column → flat [y0, y1, y0, y1, …] intervals. */
    type Intervals = Map<number, number[]>;
    function mark(map: Intervals, x0: number, x1: number, z0: number, z1: number, y0: number, y1: number): void {
      y0 = Math.max(MIN_Y, y0); y1 = Math.min(MAX_Y, y1);
      if (y1 < y0) return;
      for (let z = Math.max(0, z0); z <= Math.min(Z - 1, z1); z++)
        for (let x = Math.max(0, x0); x <= Math.min(X - 1, x1); x++) {
          const c = z * X + x;
          let list = map.get(c);
          if (!list) map.set(c, list = []);
          list.push(y0, y1);
        }
    }
    function within(list: number[], y: number): boolean {
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 2) if (y >= list[i] && y <= list[i + 1]) return true;
      return false;
    }
    
    /** Apply the route rule (see the file header). A structure that breaks it
     *  anywhere is removed whole; terrain is cut. `route` marks the route's own
     *  solid cells. */
    function clearRoute(b: Builder, course: ParkourCourse, route: Uint8Array): void {
      const hard: Intervals = new Map(), inner: Intervals = new Map(), band: Intervals = new Map();
      for (const s of course.spaces) {
        const B = s.box;
        mark(inner, B.x0, B.x1, B.z0, B.z1, B.y0 - 1, B.y1);
        if (s.pad) {
          mark(hard, B.x0, B.x1, B.z0, B.z1, B.y0, B.y1 + 2);
          mark(hard, B.x0 - 1, B.x1 + 1, B.z0 - 1, B.z1 + 1, s.lo - 3, B.y1 + 2);
        } else {
          mark(hard, B.x0 - 1, B.x1 + 1, B.z0 - 1, B.z1 + 1, s.lo - 3, s.hi + 5);
        }
        mark(band, B.x0 - 5, B.x1 + 5, B.z0 - 5, B.z1 + 5, s.lo - 3, s.hi);
      }
      const { cells, owner } = b;
      // A pad's own support — the solid column straight down from under its
      // surface — is part of the pad, not something beside the jump.
      const support = new Uint8Array(cells.length);
      for (const p of course.platforms) {
        const fx0 = Math.floor(p.x - p.width / 2), fz0 = Math.floor(p.z - p.depth / 2);
        for (let x = fx0; x < fx0 + p.width; x++)
          for (let z = fz0; z < fz0 + p.depth; z++)
            for (let y = p.y - 2; y > MIN_Y; y--) {
              const k = key(x, y, z);
              if (!SOLID[cells[k]] && !route[k]) break;
              support[k] = 1;
            }
      }
      const doomed = new Set<number>();
      /** A cell that breaks the rule: terrain is cut, a structure condemned. */
      const breaks = (k: number): void => {
        if (owner[k]) doomed.add(owner[k]);
        else cells[k] = Block.Air;
      };
      // Removing a structure can bare a top underneath it, so judge again after.
      for (let pass = 0; pass < 4; pass++) {
        doomed.clear();
        for (const [c, list] of inner)
          for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 2)
            for (let y = list[i]; y <= list[i + 1]; y++) {
              const k = (y - MIN_Y) * LAYER + c;
              if (cells[k] && !PHYSICAL[cells[k]] && !support[k]) breaks(k);
            }
        for (const [c, list] of hard)
          for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 2)
            for (let y = list[i]; y <= list[i + 1]; y++) {
              const k = (y - MIN_Y) * LAYER + c;
              if (PHYSICAL[cells[k]] && !support[k]) breaks(k);
            }
        // Standable tops inside the band, judged top down so the block under a
        // removed one is judged as the new top.
        for (const [c, list] of band) {
          let lo = MAX_Y, hi = MIN_Y;
          for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 2) { lo = Math.min(lo, list[i]); hi = Math.max(hi, list[i + 1]); }
          for (let y = hi; y >= lo; y--) {
            const k = (y - MIN_Y) * LAYER + c;
            if (!SOLID[cells[k]] || support[k] || !within(list, y)) continue;
            const above = k + LAYER;
            const covered = y < MAX_Y && (route[above] || SOLID[cells[above]]);
            if (!covered) breaks(k);
          }
        }
        if (!doomed.size) break;
        for (let k = 0; k < cells.length; k++) {
          if (!cells[k] || !doomed.has(owner[k])) continue;
          // A pad's support column is never taken out with the structure round it.
          if (support[k]) owner[k] = 0;
          else { cells[k] = Block.Air; owner[k] = 0; }
        }
      }
    }
    
    /** Sweep away anything left floating on its own: groups of touching blocks
     *  that are small and touch nothing on the route. */
    function sweepStrays(cells: Uint8Array, route: Uint8Array): void {
      const seen = new Uint8Array(cells.length);
      const stack: number[] = [], group: number[] = [];
      for (let start = 0; start < cells.length; start++) {
        if (!cells[start] || seen[start] || route[start]) continue;
        stack.length = 0; group.length = 0;
        stack.push(start); seen[start] = 1;
        let anchored = false;
        while (stack.length) {
          const k = stack.pop()!;
          group.push(k);
          const x = k % X, z = ((k - x) / X) % Z, y = Math.floor(k / LAYER);
          const next = [x > 0 ? k - 1 : -1, x < X - 1 ? k + 1 : -1, z > 0 ? k - X : -1, z < Z - 1 ? k + X : -1,
            y > 0 ? k - LAYER : -1, y < H - 1 ? k + LAYER : -1];
          for (const n of next) {
            if (n < 0 || seen[n] || !cells[n]) continue;
            if (route[n]) { anchored = true; continue; }
            seen[n] = 1;
            stack.push(n);
          }
          if (group.length >= STRAY_LIMIT) anchored = true;
        }
        if (!anchored) for (const k of group) cells[k] = Block.Air;
      }
    }
    
    // ── Lair and sanctuary ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    /** The mountain behind the start, with the dragon's cave in it. */
    function lair(b: Builder, course: ParkourCourse): void {
      const P = b.pal, s = course.start, sy = s.y, cz = 11;
      const ground = sy - 14;
      for (let z = 0; z <= cz; z++)
        for (let x = CX - 30; x <= CX + 30; x++) {
          const v = (x - CX) / 30, u = (cz - z) / 14;
          const h = Math.round(sy + 14 - v * v * 16 + u * 4);
          if (h < ground) continue;
          for (let y = ground - Math.round((1 - Math.abs(v)) * 6); y <= h; y++)
            b.put(x, y, z, y === h ? P.soil : (h - y) % 5 === 4 ? P.brick : P.rock);
        }
      for (const x of [CX - 16, CX + 16]) b.tree(x, sy + 14 - Math.round(((x - CX) / 30) ** 2 * 16) + 3, 6, 1);
      // The cave: a great arched mouth and the dragon's hoard.
      for (let z = 0; z <= cz; z++)
        for (let x = CX - 8; x <= CX + 8; x++)
          for (let y = sy - 1; y <= sy + 10; y++) {
            const dx = x - CX, arch = sy + 6 + Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 64 - dx * dx)) * .5;
            if (y > arch) continue;
            if (y === sy - 1) b.put(x, y, z, (x + z) % 3 === 0 ? P.trim : P.rock);
            else b.clear(x, y, z);
          }
      for (let z = 1; z <= 5; z++) for (let x = CX - 6; x <= CX + 6; x++) {
        const h = Math.max(0, 2 - Math.floor(Math.hypot((x - CX) / 3, (z - 3) / 1.5)));
        for (let y = sy; y < sy + h; y++) b.put(x, y, z, (x + y) % 2 ? Block.ManorGilt : Block.GildedVaultBrick);
      }
      for (const x of [CX - 9, CX + 9]) { b.put(x, sy, cz, P.lamp); b.put(x, sy + 5, cz + 1, P.lamp); }
    }
    
    /** A walled courtyard round the finish, with its podium. */
    function sanctuary(b: Builder, course: ParkourCourse): void {
      const P = b.pal, f = course.finish, fx = Math.floor(f.x), fz = Math.floor(f.z), fy = f.y;
      const z0 = fz - 2, z1 = Math.min(Z - 3, fz + 24), hx = 12;
      // Its rock starts at the finish pad's front edge: nothing here reaches back
      // under the last jump.
      for (let z = z0; z <= z1 + 1; z++)
        for (let x = fx - hx - 2; x <= fx + hx + 2; x++) {
          const d = Math.max(Math.abs(x - fx) / (hx + 2), Math.abs(z - (z0 + z1) / 2) / ((z1 - z0) / 2 + 1));
          const under = Math.round(10 * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - d * d))) + 1;
          for (let y = fy - 1 - under; y < fy - 1; y++) b.put(x, y, z, P.rock);
        }
      for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++)
        for (let x = fx - hx; x <= fx + hx; x++)
          b.put(x, fy - 1, z, (Math.abs(x - fx) + (z - fz)) % 4 === 0 ? P.trim : P.brick);
      for (let y = fy; y <= fy + 4; y++) {
        for (let z = z0 + 2; z <= z1; z++) { b.put(fx - hx, y, z, P.stone); b.put(fx + hx, y, z, P.stone); }
        for (let x = fx - hx; x <= fx + hx; x++) b.put(x, y, z1, P.stone);
      }
      for (let z = z0 + 2; z <= z1; z += 2) { b.put(fx - hx, fy + 5, z, P.trim); b.put(fx + hx, fy + 5, z, P.trim); }
      for (const [x, z] of [[fx - hx, z1], [fx + hx, z1], [fx - hx, z0 + 2], [fx + hx, z0 + 2]])
        b.tower(x, z, 2.2, fy, fy + 9, { roof: P.roof });
      // The gate the finishers run through.
      for (const x of [fx - 5, fx + 5]) { b.fill(x, x, fy - 4, fy + 7, z0 - 1, z0 - 1, P.trim); b.put(x, fy + 8, z0 - 1, P.lamp); }
      b.fill(fx - 5, fx + 5, fy + 7, fy + 7, z0 - 1, z0 - 1, Block.GildedVaultBrick);
      b.fill(fx - 3, fx + 3, fy + 8, fy + 8, z0 - 1, z0 - 1, Block.GildedVaultBrick);
      b.put(fx, fy + 9, z0 - 1, Block.RuneGlass);
      for (const x of [fx - 4, fx + 4]) b.banner(x, fy + 6, z0 - 1, 3, P.cloth);
      // The podium: gold in the middle, silver either side, bronze either side
      // of those — the same on both sides.
      const mats = [Block.ManorGilt, Block.ManorPewter, Block.ManorPewter, Block.ManorBronzeTile];
      course.podium.forEach((spot, i) => {
        const x = Math.floor(spot.x), z = Math.floor(spot.z);
        for (const sx of [x, 2 * fx - x]) b.fill(sx - 1, sx + 1, fy, spot.y - 1, z - 1, z + 1, mats[i]);
      });
      // A fountain behind the podium, and lamps along the walls.
      const bz = Math.min(z1 - 5, Math.floor(course.podium[0].z) + 7);
      b.fill(fx - 3, fx + 3, fy, fy, bz - 2, bz + 2, P.trim);
      b.fill(fx - 2, fx + 2, fy, fy, bz - 1, bz + 1, P.liquid);
      b.fill(fx, fx, fy, fy + 3, bz, bz, P.trim);
      b.put(fx, fy + 4, bz, P.glow);
      for (let z = z0 + 4; z < z1; z += 5) { b.lampPost(fx - hx + 2, fy, z, 2); b.lampPost(fx + hx - 2, fy, z, 2); }
    }
    
    // ── The venue ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    
    /** Scenery and route for a seed, stamped and packed. */
    export function parkourVenue(seed: number): ParkourVenue {
      const course = parkourCourse(seed);
      const b = new Builder(seed ^ 0x5ce4e, parkourTheme(seed).palette);
      course.sections.forEach((sec, index) => {
        const pads = course.platforms.filter(p => p.section === index);
        if (!pads.length) return;
        const ys = pads.map(p => p.y);
        const area: Area = {
          index, sec, pads,
          x0: Math.min(...pads.map(p => foot(p).x0)), x1: Math.max(...pads.map(p => foot(p).x1)),
          z0: Math.min(sec.z0, ...pads.map(p => foot(p).z0)), z1: Math.max(sec.z1, ...pads.map(p => foot(p).z1)),
          lo: Math.min(...ys), hi: Math.max(...ys),
        };
        BUILDERS[sec.piece](b, area, course);
      });
      b.mirror();
      const route = new Uint8Array(LAYER * H);
      for (const c of course.cells) if (c.block !== Block.Air && b.inside(c.x, c.y, c.z)) route[key(c.x, c.y, c.z)] = 1;
      clearRoute(b, course, route);
      // The lair and the sanctuary are built round the start and the finish
      // themselves, after the route rule: they are where you stand, not jumps.
      lair(b, course);
      sanctuary(b, course);
      b.mirror();
      for (const c of course.cells) if (b.inside(c.x, c.y, c.z)) b.cells[key(c.x, c.y, c.z)] = c.block;
      sweepStrays(b.cells, route);
      return pack(b.cells);
    }
